# whisper.txt — the transcription check on the three full chapters, both voices (11/11/2026)
#
# Every sentence clip was transcribed and compared with the sentence on the page, before the clips were laid out.
# Model: OpenAI whisper small.en, int8, on the CPU. huggingface.co is closed to this session, so faster-whisper could not
# fetch its weights; the same small.en weights were taken from the sherpa-onnx project's GitHub release (ONNX, int8) and
# run with sherpa-onnx. Word timestamps (for the two sentences split around an unvoiced Lithuanian word, and for the
# seek-point check) come from a zipformer transducer from the same release.
# Scoring: word error rate against the page sentence; Tony's respelled names are excused (whisper writes "2 NAS" for
# too-nas), digits are read as words, British spellings as American. A clip over 10% or missing its last words was
# re-synthesized with the dashes read as commas, then split at the dash; the best reading was kept. Clips of five words
# or fewer are judged by their letters (whisper hears "Warm." alone as "worms").
# "BAD" lines that remain are, on inspection, the names and the one- or two-word clips, not misreadings.


====================================================================================================
